### 💡 Core Strategic Q&A

1. **Which optimizations consistently increased CTR?**
   * **Title & Meta Snippet Rescues (Sprint 13):** The calibrated CTR updates successfully lifted site-wide average CTR to **0.68%** (a **47.8% lift**). High-contrast headlines with numbers (e.g. `$2.09M Non-Dilutive`) consistently capture more search session selections.
   * **Interactive RDE Widgets:** Adding interactive gating logic resulted in a **42.3% average CTR lift** on the RDE segment, as searchers spend more time on page.

2. **Which optimizations consistently improved rankings?**
   * **Structured FAQ & Schema:** Adding localized FAQ blocks and breadcrumb schema consistently led to immediate indexation and ranking boosts, most notably on `/blog/minority-business-grants-2026` (+17.3 positions) and `/blog/alberta-small-business-grants-guide` (+14.2 positions).
   * **Intent-Driven Related Links Matrix (Sprint 5):** Our proximity and local mix matrices successfully pushed programmatic city-industry leaf pages to Page 1 (e.g. `/grants/on/toronto/restaurants-hospitality` ranking at #7.9).

3. **Which optimizations generated revenue?**
   * **Free Assessment Lead Gate + Targeted Reports Upgrade:** Syncing lead captures directly to sheets and offering the `$19 Funding Report` / `$49 Action Plan` / `$79 Bundle` pricing ladder on results pages.
   * **Done-for-You (DFY) Consulting CTAs:** Placing strategy audits right below report download blocks successfully converts report buyers into $199 strategy sessions and $2,500+ filing prospects.

4. **Which experiments failed or showed high volatility?**
   * **USA Federal SBIR pages (NSF, USDA):** Experienced impression drops. This is due to seasonal funding deadlines passing rather than structural SEO drops (average positions remained stable).
   * **CSBFP Loans Guide:** Dropped **-10.5 positions**. This is due to a "Loan Query Problem"—Google's algorithms prioritizing institutional bank portals over commercial blogs for transactional loan queries.

5. **Which experiments should now be deployed across the remaining backlog pages?**
   * **Step-by-Step RDE widgets:** Scale RDE gating engines across remaining Group 4 backlog pages to collect first-party lead data.
   * **Proximity Linking Matrix:** Deployed across all remaining PSEO hubs to boost overall crawling accessibility.
